# M04 - Review and checking campaign

## Module contract and selection

| Module type | Decision | Instance IDs | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| M04 | SELECTED | MI-NORMAL-001-CHECK, MI-FL2-S1-001-REVIEW, MI-FL2-S2-001-REMAIN, MI-NORMAL-002-CHECK, MI-FL2-S1-002-REVIEW, MI-FL2-S2-002-REMAIN, MI-NORMAL-003-CHECK, MI-FL2-S1-003-REVIEW, MI-FL2-S2-003-REMAIN, MI-NORMAL-004-CHECK, MI-FL2-S1-004-REVIEW, MI-FL2-S2-004-REMAIN, MI-NORMAL-005-CHECK, MI-FL2-S1-005-REVIEW, MI-FL2-S2-005-REMAIN, MI-NORMAL-006-CHECK, MI-FL2-S1-006-REVIEW, MI-FL2-S2-006-REMAIN, MI-NORMAL-007-CHECK, MI-FL2-S1-007-REVIEW, MI-FL2-S2-007-REMAIN, MI-NORMAL-008-CHECK, MI-FL2-S1-008-REVIEW, MI-FL2-S2-008-REMAIN, MI-NORMAL-009-CHECK, MI-FL2-S1-009-REVIEW, MI-FL2-S2-009-REMAIN, MI-NORMAL-010-CHECK, MI-FL2-S1-010-REVIEW | SELECTED: Run all selected non-producer checking paths for one gate against one shared reviewable input, in parallel where independent, then return one complete result set. Smoke, focused checks, review, observation, fan-out, and join are internal campaign parts, not separate top-level modules. This product's exact identities, trust, real native/service proof and recovery require the configured occurrences; no extra runner or release ceremony. |

Run all selected non-producer checking paths for one gate against one shared reviewable input, in parallel where
independent, then return one complete result set. Smoke, focused checks, review, observation, fan-out,
and join are internal campaign parts, not separate top-level modules.

## Public interface and compatibility boundary

same frozen tip + selected review/check paths -> one complete result pool, no acceptance or repair. Concrete values and return targets belong to the owning step's configured instance. Private changes do not alter public semantics; update actual consumers for a real contract change.

## Rules, process, recipe actions, and allowed variations

P01–P15 own shared rules. Semantic choices in recipe verbs remain ROOT decisions; test_root only authors declared lane-local contracts. Workers mechanically execute supplied choices and return any missing meaning. Reviewer findings remain open within assigned boundaries. Each governing card cites all actions; member cards cite only their ordered subset. Parameters may select bounded source/test paths and optional profiles, never weaken acceptance or change authority.

| Order | Recipe action ID | Project-specific action/process | Allowed parameterization | Decision owner |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | M04-A1 | Verify every path consumes the same declared frozen input or record a real dependency that forces a different subgroup; never compare results from silently different tips. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 2 | M04-A2 | Put the cheapest decisive check in the M04-A3 parallel group unless a named output dependency requires it first. Do not serialize it merely to discover an ordinary failure early. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 3 | M04-A3 | For all remaining independent review, deterministic-check, and observer paths, allocate disjoint writable roots and launch every member before awaiting any member. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 4 | M04-A4 | Require each path to finish its assigned surface and return its complete findings/results. A deterministic multi-check path checkpoints each runnable unit and continues after ordinary failure; stop early only for a named failed prerequisite or the exact R23 containment exception. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 5 | M04-A5 | Preserve each required path result; do not let one writer overwrite another's output. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 6 | M04-A6 | Join exactly once. Deduplicate equivalent findings, preserve disagreements, and bind the combined set to the frozen input and campaign ID. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 7 | M04-A7 | Publish the complete result set, unit checkpoints, and reused/invalidated PASS credit to M05. Do not repair, accept, or rerun inside M04. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 8 | M04-A8 | On an affected-repair repeat, keep the same declared functional roles and cover the repair surface, violated invariants, and only dependencies invalidated by the new tip. Reuse each invocation when available and still selected; otherwise dispatch that role through its structured handoff. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |

- A failed prerequisite may skip only the dependent path, and R23 may contain live harm. Record the
  reason and incomplete path. An ordinary product failure does not cancel another feasible path.
- Support/report faults use M05 administrative/support classification, do not become product findings,
  and cannot delay the campaign's M05 handoff when required product results are decidable.
- Campaign completion always proceeds to M05, including mixed, failing, incomplete, or disagreeing results.
- A reviewer may justify minimum adjacent inspection needed to understand behavior or substantiate a
  finding. Broader investigation requires a recommendation to its owning authority and a separately
  authorized card; cross-lane expansion returns to ROOT and is not an implicit extension of M04.
- A `MI-FL2-S1-*` M04 occurrence performs the declared independent frozen-tip review and only the
  minimal smoke evidence not already produced by its separate `MI-FL2-S1-*` M02 occurrence; it must
  not import the `MI-NORMAL-*` M04 broad deterministic campaign. A `MI-FL2-S2-*` M04 occurrence consumes the progress-bound
  checkpoint input map and runs only failed, unresolved, affected, uncertain, or uncredited units.
  Both use their required prefix, remain distinct from the normal M04 occurrence, and state the saved work.

## Configured module instances

### MI-NORMAL-001-CHECK - M04: Freeze the builder and qualify the development boundary / NORMAL / CHECK

#### Purpose

Review class INITIAL_IMPLEMENTATION; frozen accepted producer tip or declared integrated release. Review changes only when consumed source/contract/evidence changes.

ROOT preselects the stage-owned scoped tests plus one independent frozen-tip review; launch independent paths before waits, complete feasible collection and join once. No repairs or acceptance inside this campaign. Review findings are open but must violate a requirement or invalidate required evidence. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Coverage

DEL-001; REQ-001, REQ-027, REQ-028, REQ-029; STEP-001 NORMAL.

#### Selection basis

Required review and checking campaign
 boundary; Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-NORMAL-001-CHECK-REVIEW=reviewer, CARD-NORMAL-001-CHECK-CHECK=routine

#### Preconditions

MI-NORMAL-001-ASSETS.typed output satisfying M04 public input is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

#### Inputs

MI-NORMAL-001-ASSETS.typed output satisfying M04 public input; REQ-001, REQ-027, REQ-028, REQ-029; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Use this step's normal BUILD domain brief and root Section3 observations; this is a reference, not permission to infer new requirements.

#### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-001-CHECK.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`development/dogfood/`, `development/test-tools/`, disposable
`development/test-runs/` and `development/evidence/`; deliberately staged candidate
configuration/test assets in `P` only. Reference source remains unchanged. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. Assembly cwd: python -m unittest discover -s development/test-tools/tests -p test_*.py; standard-library resolver/isolation/control tests and exact native readbacks. Does not depend on STEP-002 product pyproject or product pytest assets. Native all-off observations belong to M09. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

0.5–2 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-FL2-S1-001-REVIEW - M04: Freeze the builder and qualify the development boundary / FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_1 / REVIEW

#### Purpose

Review class AFFECTED_REPAIR; frozen accepted producer tip or declared integrated release. Review changes only when consumed source/contract/evidence changes.

ROOT preselects the stage-owned scoped tests plus one independent frozen-tip review; launch independent paths before waits, complete feasible collection and join once. No repairs or acceptance inside this campaign. Review findings are open but must violate a requirement or invalidate required evidence. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained.

#### Coverage

DEL-001; REQ-001, REQ-027, REQ-028, REQ-029; STEP-001 FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_1.

#### Selection basis

Required review and checking campaign
 boundary; Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-FL2-S1-001-REVIEW-REVIEW=reviewer

#### Preconditions

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S1-001-REVIEW.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`development/dogfood/`, `development/test-tools/`, disposable
`development/test-runs/` and `development/evidence/`; deliberately staged candidate
configuration/test assets in `P` only. Reference source remains unchanged. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. Read-only independent review of the accepted repair tip, complete objective, protected behavior and retained changed-source/motivating-test smoke. Do not rerun retained smoke or broaden into a stage campaign. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

0.5–2 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-FL2-S2-001-REMAIN - M04: Freeze the builder and qualify the development boundary / FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_2 / REMAIN

#### Purpose

Review class AFFECTED_REPAIR; frozen accepted producer tip or declared integrated release. Review changes only when consumed source/contract/evidence changes.

ROOT preselects the stage-owned scoped tests plus one independent frozen-tip review; launch independent paths before waits, complete feasible collection and join once. No repairs or acceptance inside this campaign. Review findings are open but must violate a requirement or invalidate required evidence. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision.

#### Coverage

DEL-001; REQ-001, REQ-027, REQ-028, REQ-029; STEP-001 FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_2.

all accepted integrated affected repair exits plus bound checkpoint is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S2-001-REMAIN.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`development/dogfood/`, `development/test-tools/`, disposable
`development/test-runs/` and `development/evidence/`; deliberately staged candidate
configuration/test assets in `P` only. Reference source remains unchanged. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. Assembly cwd: python -m unittest discover -s development/test-tools/tests -p test_*.py; standard-library resolver/isolation/control tests and exact native readbacks. Does not depend on STEP-002 product pyproject or product pytest assets. Native all-off observations belong to M09. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

0.5–2 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–5 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S1-003-REVIEW.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

minimum implementations in `P/src/memory_harness/`, a minimal reviewed
template, `P/tests/integration/`, candidate `P/harness/orchestrator_harness/`
bootstrap/resume/config/provider seams, and assigned synthetic service resources.
These candidate changes never modify the frozen builder. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. Read-only independent review of the accepted repair tip, complete objective, protected behavior and retained changed-source/motivating-test smoke. Do not rerun retained smoke or broaden into a stage campaign. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–5 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-FL2-S2-003-REMAIN - M04: Prove the thin end-to-end product slice / FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_2 / REMAIN

#### Purpose

Review class AFFECTED_REPAIR; frozen accepted producer tip or declared integrated release. Review changes only when consumed source/contract/evidence changes.

ROOT preselects the stage-owned scoped tests plus one independent frozen-tip review; launch independent paths before waits, complete feasible collection and join once. No repairs or acceptance inside this campaign. Review findings are open but must violate a requirement or invalidate required evidence. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision.

#### Coverage

DEL-003; REQ-002, REQ-003, REQ-004, REQ-019, REQ-020, REQ-022, REQ-025, REQ-026; STEP-003 FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_2.

Required review and checking campaign
 boundary; Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision.

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-FL2-S2-003-REMAIN-REVIEW=reviewer, CARD-FL2-S2-003-REMAIN-CHECK=routine

all accepted integrated affected repair exits plus bound checkpoint is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

all accepted integrated affected repair exits plus bound checkpoint; REQ-002, REQ-003, REQ-004, REQ-019, REQ-020, REQ-022, REQ-025, REQ-026; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. #### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S2-003-REMAIN.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

minimum implementations in `P/src/memory_harness/`, a minimal reviewed
template, `P/tests/integration/`, candidate `P/harness/orchestrator_harness/`
bootstrap/resume/config/provider seams, and assigned synthetic service resources.
These candidate changes never modify the frozen builder. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. cwd corresponding P worktree; uv run --frozen python -m pytest tests/integration -q; one isolated process per useful directory under Section10 scheduling, no -x. Tests absent today are M03 assets, not current runnable claims.  Run only checkpoint-selected necessary units under P04; never automatically execute full command selection. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–5 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S2-004-REMAIN.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; only disjoint adapter writes overlap. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/experience/`, `P/tests/experience/`, focused
EverOS extensions/tests only under `P/harness/vendor/everos/`. No shared schema,
Atlas module, common fixtures or package-manifest edits without ROOT reassignment. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. cwd corresponding P worktree; uv run --frozen python -m pytest tests/experience -q; one isolated process per useful directory under Section10 scheduling, no -x. Tests absent today are M03 assets, not current runnable claims.  Run only checkpoint-selected necessary units under P04; never automatically execute full command selection. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-005-CHECK.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; only disjoint adapter writes overlap. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/procedures/`, `P/src/memory_harness/atlas/`,
`P/tests/{procedures,atlas}/`, scoped index/bootstrap definitions and operator
documentation. Any vendored MongoDB changes stay in its candidate copy and get
their own affected upstream tests; none is presumed necessary. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. cwd corresponding P worktree; uv run --frozen python -m pytest tests/procedures tests/atlas -q; one isolated process per useful directory under Section10 scheduling, no -x. Tests absent today are M03 assets, not current runnable claims. Real service/native claims use the M09 selected scenario and actual native receipts; local fixtures cannot pass them.  Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; only disjoint adapter writes overlap. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

`P/src/memory_harness/procedures/`, `P/src/memory_harness/atlas/`,
`P/tests/{procedures,atlas}/`, scoped index/bootstrap definitions and operator
documentation. Any vendored MongoDB changes stay in its candidate copy and get
their own affected upstream tests; none is presumed necessary. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-006-CHECK.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/{search,templates,apc}/`, five reviewed template
assets, `P/tests/{search,templates,apc}/`; candidate harness delegation adapter
only through ROOT-owned seam changes. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. cwd corresponding P worktree; uv run --frozen python -m pytest tests/search tests/templates tests/apc -q; one isolated process per useful directory under Section10 scheduling, no -x. Tests absent today are M03 assets, not current runnable claims.   Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S1-006-REVIEW.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/{search,templates,apc}/`, five reviewed template
assets, `P/tests/{search,templates,apc}/`; candidate harness delegation adapter
only through ROOT-owned seam changes. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. Read-only independent review of the accepted repair tip, complete objective, protected behavior and retained changed-source/motivating-test smoke. Do not rerun retained smoke or broaden into a stage campaign. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-FL2-S2-006-REMAIN - M04: Complete fixed search, templates and APC / FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_2 / REMAIN

#### Purpose

Review class AFFECTED_REPAIR; frozen accepted producer tip or declared integrated release. Review changes only when consumed source/contract/evidence changes.

ROOT preselects the stage-owned scoped tests plus one independent frozen-tip review; launch independent paths before waits, complete feasible collection and join once. No repairs or acceptance inside this campaign. Review findings are open but must violate a requirement or invalidate required evidence. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision.

#### Coverage

DEL-006; REQ-002, REQ-010, REQ-011, REQ-012, REQ-013, REQ-014, REQ-015; STEP-006 FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_2.

#### Selection basis

Required review and checking campaign
 boundary; Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-FL2-S2-006-REMAIN-REVIEW=reviewer, CARD-FL2-S2-006-REMAIN-CHECK=routine

#### Preconditions

all accepted integrated affected repair exits plus bound checkpoint is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

#### Inputs

all accepted integrated affected repair exits plus bound checkpoint; REQ-002, REQ-010, REQ-011, REQ-012, REQ-013, REQ-014, REQ-015; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Use this step's normal BUILD domain brief and root Section3 observations; this is a reference, not permission to infer new requirements.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S2-006-REMAIN.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/{search,templates,apc}/`, five reviewed template
assets, `P/tests/{search,templates,apc}/`; candidate harness delegation adapter
only through ROOT-owned seam changes. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. cwd corresponding P worktree; uv run --frozen python -m pytest tests/search tests/templates tests/apc -q; one isolated process per useful directory under Section10 scheduling, no -x. Tests absent today are M03 assets, not current runnable claims.  Run only checkpoint-selected necessary units under P04; never automatically execute full command selection. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. #### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S2-007-REMAIN.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/{context,composition}/`, candidate harness
bootstrap/resume/setup/provider payloads, product ROOT provider configuration,
`P/tests/{context,composition,features,security}/`. Shared config edits are serialized. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. cwd corresponding P worktree; uv run --frozen python -m pytest tests/context tests/composition tests/features tests/security -q; one isolated process per useful directory under Section10 scheduling, no -x. Tests absent today are M03 assets, not current runnable claims.  Run only checkpoint-selected necessary units under P04; never automatically execute full command selection. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-FL2-S2-008-REMAIN - M04: Finish recovery, accounting, snapshots and operator usability / FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_2 / REMAIN

#### Purpose

Review class AFFECTED_REPAIR; frozen accepted producer tip or declared integrated release. Review changes only when consumed source/contract/evidence changes.

ROOT preselects the stage-owned scoped tests plus one independent frozen-tip review; launch independent paths before waits, complete feasible collection and join once. #### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-009-CHECK.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

disposable fixture repositories and evidence under the wave root;
no product source changes during collection. Product defects return to their
outer implementation owner and a newly pinned candidate wave. Additionally P/src/memory_harness/cli/demo.py and P/tests/native implement the required synthetic demo entrypoint, not a benchmark runner. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. cwd corresponding P worktree; uv run --frozen python -m pytest tests/integration -q; one isolated process per useful directory under Section10 scheduling, no -x. Tests absent today are M03 assets, not current runnable claims. Real service/native claims use the M09 selected scenario and actual native receipts; local fixtures cannot pass them.  Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-FL2-S1-009-REVIEW - M04: Integrated candidate verification and synthetic demonstration / FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_1 / REVIEW

#### Purpose

Review class AFFECTED_REPAIR; frozen accepted producer tip or declared integrated release. Review changes only when consumed source/contract/evidence changes.

ROOT preselects the stage-owned scoped tests plus one independent frozen-tip review; launch independent paths before waits, complete feasible collection and join once. No repairs or acceptance inside this campaign. Review findings are open but must violate a requirement or invalidate required evidence. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S1-009-REVIEW.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

disposable fixture repositories and evidence under the wave root;
no product source changes during collection. Product defects return to their
outer implementation owner and a newly pinned candidate wave. Additionally P/src/memory_harness/cli/demo.py and P/tests/native implement the required synthetic demo entrypoint, not a benchmark runner. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. Read-only independent review of the accepted repair tip, complete objective, protected behavior and retained changed-source/motivating-test smoke. Do not rerun retained smoke or broaden into a stage campaign. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-FL2-S2-009-REMAIN - M04: Integrated candidate verification and synthetic demonstration / FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_2 / REMAIN

#### Purpose

Review class AFFECTED_REPAIR; frozen accepted producer tip or declared integrated release. Review changes only when consumed source/contract/evidence changes.

ROOT preselects the stage-owned scoped tests plus one independent frozen-tip review; launch independent paths before waits, complete feasible collection and join once. No repairs or acceptance inside this campaign. Review findings are open but must violate a requirement or invalidate required evidence. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision.

#### Coverage

DEL-009; REQ-020, REQ-022, REQ-024, REQ-025, REQ-026, REQ-027, REQ-028, REQ-029, REQ-030; STEP-009 FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_2.

#### Selection basis

Required review and checking campaign
 boundary; Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-FL2-S2-009-REMAIN-REVIEW=reviewer, CARD-FL2-S2-009-REMAIN-CHECK=routine

#### Preconditions

all accepted integrated affected repair exits plus bound checkpoint is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

#### Inputs

all accepted integrated affected repair exits plus bound checkpoint; REQ-020, REQ-022, REQ-024, REQ-025, REQ-026, REQ-027, REQ-028, REQ-029, REQ-030; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Use this step's normal BUILD domain brief and root Section3 observations; this is a reference, not permission to infer new requirements.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S2-009-REMAIN.complete campaign pool; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

disposable fixture repositories and evidence under the wave root;
no product source changes during collection. Product defects return to their
outer implementation owner and a newly pinned candidate wave. Additionally P/src/memory_harness/cli/demo.py and P/tests/native implement the required synthetic demo entrypoint, not a benchmark runner. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Publish one complete truthful pool from every feasible selected check and independent review, including FAIL/TIMEOUT/blocked/unrun and retained credit. No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. cwd corresponding P worktree; uv run --frozen python -m pytest tests/integration -q; one isolated process per useful directory under Section10 scheduling, no -x. Tests absent today are M03 assets, not current runnable claims.  Run only checkpoint-selected necessary units under P04; never automatically execute full command selection. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

No repair or product verdict inside collection; ordinary product failure does not prevent this factual handoff. Focused documentation/operator check: cwd P, uv run --frozen python -m pytest tests/cli tests/scope -q; inspect executable runbook commands against actual CLI help. Full safeguard belongs only to M07. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

0.5–1 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M04 uses 2 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.
